8a1(1): @#/ O /par du mdzad pas/rgyal pos khab grong khyer chen po 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan+da ka gnas pa na /dge slong dang dge slong ma dang /dge bsnyen dang dge ba

12a1(1): @#/ O /de nas de 'di snyam du sems lags te /rgyal po la blta ba dang /phyag bya ba'i phyir bdag song du zin kyang /bdag lta ba 'am phyag bya ba yang dka' bar gyur na /gsol pa lta ci smos/

12b7(1): rol tu phyin pa'i tshad de la bu dang /smon lam rnams kyi tshad de lta bu dang /smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshad da lta bu dang /thabs kyi tshad de lta bu dang thabs la mkhas pa'i pha rol
